         │                                                                  │
         │ # List EKS clusters                                             │
         │ aws eks list-clusters                                           │
         │ aws eks describe-cluster --name anihpj-cluster                  │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # List node groups                                              │
         │ aws eks list-nodegroups --cluster-name anihpj-cluster           │
         │ aws eks describe-nodegroup --cluster-name C --nodegroup-name NG │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # Create addon (VPC CNI, CoreDNS, kube-proxy)                   │
         │ aws eks create-addon --cluster-name C --addon-name vpc-cni     │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # Check cluster auth (IRSA test)                                │
         │ aws eks get-token --cluster-name anihpj-cluster                 │
         └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    D.2  ECR Image Management
         ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ aws ecr get-login-password | docker login --username AWS \     │
         │   --password-stdin $ECR_URI                                     │
         │ aws ecr create-repository --repository-name anihpj             │
         │ aws ecr describe-repositories                                   │
         │ aws ecr list-images --repository-name anihpj                   │
         │ aws ecr batch-delete-image --repository-name anihpj \          │
         │   --image-ids imageTag=old-v1                                   │
         │ # Set lifecycle policy (keep last 10 images)                    │
         │ aws ecr put-lifecycle-policy --repository-name anihpj \        │
         │   --lifecycle-policy-text '{"rules":[{"rulePriority":1,        │
         │   "selection":{"tagStatus":"any","countType":"imageCountMoreThan",│
         │   "countNumber":10},"action":{"type":"expire"}}]}'              │
         └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    D.3  CodePipeline & CodeBuild
         ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ # Start pipeline execution                                      │
         │ aws codepipeline start-pipeline-execution --name anihpj-pipeline│
         │ aws codepipeline get-pipeline-state --name anihpj-pipeline      │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # List Build projects                                           │
         │ aws codebuild list-projects                                     │
         │ aws codebuild start-build --project-name anihpj-build           │
         │ aws codebuild batch-get-builds --ids anihpj-build:123           │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # Get build logs                                                │
         │ aws codebuild batch-get-builds --ids anihpj-build:123 \         │
         │   --query 'builds[0].logs'                                       │
         └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    D.4  CloudFormation & IaC
         ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ aws cloudformation create-stack --stack-name anihpj-eks \       │
         │   --template-body file://eks-cluster.yaml                       │
         │ aws cloudformation describe-stacks --stack-name anihpj-eks      │
         │ aws cloudformation update-stack --stack-name anihpj-eks \       │
         │   --template-body file://eks-v2.yaml                           │
         │ aws cloudformation delete-stack --stack-name anihpj-eks         │
         │ aws cloudformation detect-stack-drift --stack-name anihpj-eks  │
         │ aws cloudformation list-stack-resources --stack-name anihpj-eks│
         │ # Create change set (preview changes before applying)           │
         │ aws cloudformation create-change-set --stack-name anihpj-eks \ │
         │   --change-set-name update-nodes --template-body file://v2.yaml│
         └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    D.5  CloudWatch & Monitoring
         ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ # Get metrics                                                   │
         │ aws cloudwatch list-metrics --namespace AWS/EKS                │
         │ aws cloudwatch get-metric-statistics --namespace AWS/EC2 \      │
         │   --metric-name CPUUtilization --start-time 2024-01-01 \        │
         │   --end-time 2024-01-02 --period 300 --statistics Average      │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # Log groups and log streams                                    │
         │ aws logs describe-log-groups                                    │
         │ aws logs tail /aws/eks/anihpj-cluster/cluster --follow          │
         │ aws logs filter-log-events --log-group-name /aws/eks/C \        │
         │   --filter-pattern "ERROR"                                      │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # CloudWatch alarms                                             │
         │ aws cloudwatch describe-alarms                                   │
         │ aws cloudwatch set-alarm-state --alarm-name "HighCPU" \         │
         │   --state-value ALARM --state-reason "Testing"                  │
         └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    D.6  IAM & Security
         ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ # Who am I?                                                     │
         │ aws sts get-caller-identity                                     │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # IAM roles and policies                                        │
         │ aws iam list-roles                                              │
         │ aws iam get-role --role-name anihpj-eks-role                    │
         │ aws iam list-attached-role-policies --role-name R               │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # IRSA (IAM Roles for Service Accounts) — check OIDC            │
         │ aws eks describe-cluster --name C --query "cluster.identity.oidc"│
         │                                                                  │
         │ # Secrets Manager                                               │
         │ aws secretsmanager list-secrets                                 │
         │ aws secretsmanager get-secret-value --secret-id anihpj/db       │
         │ aws secretsmanager rotate-secret --secret-id anihpj/db          │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # KMS                                                           │
         │ aws kms list-keys                                               │
         │ aws kms encrypt --key-id alias/anihpj --plaintext "secret"      │
         └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    D.7  S3 Operations
         ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ aws s3 ls                           # List buckets              │
         │ aws s3 ls s3://anihpj-static/       # List bucket contents      │
         │ aws s3 cp index.html s3://anihpj-static/  # Upload file         │
         │ aws s3 sync ./static/ s3://anihpj-static/static/  # Sync folder│
         │ aws s3 website s3://anihpj-static --index-document index.html  │
         │ aws s3api put-bucket-policy --bucket B --policy file://pol.json│
         │ # Generate pre-signed URL (temporary access)                    │
         │ aws s3 presign s3://anihpj-static/private/file.pdf --expires 3600│
         └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    D.8  SSM & Automation
         ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ # Session Manager (no SSH needed!)                              │
         │ aws ssm start-session --target i-1234567890abcdef0             │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # Run command on EC2                                            │
         │ aws ssm send-command --document-name "AWS-RunShellScript" \     │
         │   --targets "Key=instanceids,Values=i-123" \                    │
         │   --parameters '{"commands":["df -h"]}'                         │
         │                                                                  │
         │ # Parameter Store                                               │
         │ aws ssm get-parameter --name /anihpj/prod/DB_HOST               │
         │ aws ssm put-parameter --name /anihpj/prod/LOG_LEVEL \           │
         │   --value "INFO" --type String --overwrite                      │
         │ aws ssm get-parameters-by-path --path /anihpj/prod/             │
         └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

Appendix E: YAML Template Library — anihpj Complete Stack
─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    E.1  Dockerfile (Multi-stage, Optimized) — See complete annotated version
         earlier in this document (after the Three-Layer Model section)

    E.2  docker-compose.yml (Local Development)
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ version: '3.8'                                                    │
         │ services:                                                         │
         │   db:                                                             │
         │     image: postgres:16-alpine                                     │
         │     environment:                                                  │
         │       POSTGRES_DB: anihpj                                         │
         │       POSTGRES_USER: anihpj                                       │
         │       POSTGRES_PASSWORD: devpassword                              │
         │     ports:                                                        │
         │       - "5432:5432"                                               │
         │     volumes:                                                      │
         │       - pgdata:/var/lib/postgresql/data                           │
         │                                                                   │
         │   web:                                                            │
         │     build: .                                                      │
         │     command: python manage.py runserver 0.0.0.0:8000              │
         │     volumes:                                                      │
         │       - .:/app                  # Live code reload                │
         │     ports:                                                        │
         │       - "8000:8000"                                               │
         │     depends_on:                                                   │
         │       - db                                                        │
         │     environment:                                                  │
         │       - DATABASE_URL=postgres://anihpj:devpassword@db:5432/anihpj │
         │                                                                   │
         │ volumes:                                                          │
         │   pgdata:                                                         │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    E.3  Deployment YAML (Production-Ready on EKS)
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ apiVersion: apps/v1                                               │
         │ kind: Deployment                                                  │
         │ metadata:                                                         │
         │   name: anihpj                                                    │
         │   namespace: production                                           │
         │   labels:                                                         │
         │     app: anihpj                                                   │
         │     tier: backend                                                 │
         │   annotations:                                                    │
         │     kubernetes.io/change-cause: "Deploy v2.1 — skill matching"   │
         │ spec:                                                             │
         │   replicas: 3                                                     │
         │   strategy:                                                       │
         │     type: RollingUpdate                                           │
         │     rollingUpdate:                                                │
         │       maxSurge: 1              # 1 extra Pod during rollout       │
         │       maxUnavailable: 0         # 0 downtime (keep all 3 running) │
         │   selector:                                                       │
         │     matchLabels:                                                  │
         │       app: anihpj                                                 │
         │   template:                                                       │
         │     metadata:                                                     │
         │       labels:                                                     │
         │         app: anihpj                                               │
         │         version: v2.1                                             │
         │     spec:                                                         │
         │       serviceAccountName: anihpj-sa                               │
         │       containers:                                                 │
         │       - name: anihpj                                              │
         │         image: 123456789012.dkr.ecr.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/      │
         │                anihpj:v2.1                                        │
         │         ports:                                                    │
         │         - containerPort: 8000                                     │
         │           protocol: TCP                                           │
         │         env:                                                      │
         │         - name: DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE                            │
         │           value: "anihpj.settings.production"                     │
         │         - name: DB_HOST                                           │
         │           valueFrom:                                              │
         │             secretKeyRef:                                         │
         │               name: anihpj-secrets                                │
         │               key: db-host                                        │
         │         envFrom:                                                  │
         │         - configMapRef:                                           │
         │             name: anihpj-config                                   │
         │         resources:                                                │
         │           requests:                                               │
         │             cpu: "250m"                                           │
         │             memory: "256Mi"                                       │
         │           limits:                                                 │
         │             cpu: "500m"                                           │
         │             memory: "512Mi"                                       │
         │         livenessProbe:                                            │
         │           httpGet:                                                │
         │             path: /health/                                        │
         │             port: 8000                                            │
         │           initialDelaySeconds: 30                                 │
         │           periodSeconds: 10                                       │
         │         readinessProbe:                                           │
         │           httpGet:                                                │
         │             path: /health/                                        │
         │             port: 8000                                            │
         │           initialDelaySeconds: 5                                  │
         │           periodSeconds: 5                                        │
         │         securityContext:                                          │
         │           runAsNonRoot: true                                      │
         │           runAsUser: 1000                                         │
         │           allowPrivilegeEscalation: false                         │
         │           capabilities:                                           │
         │             drop: ["ALL"]                                         │
         │         volumeMounts:                                             │
         │         - name: static-files                                      │
         │           mountPath: /app/staticfiles                             │
         │       volumes:                                                    │
         │       - name: static-files                                        │
         │         emptyDir: {}                                              │
         │       affinity:                                                   │
         │         podAntiAffinity:                                          │
         │           preferredDuringSchedulingIgnoredDuringExecution:        │
         │           - weight: 100                                           │
         │             podAffinityTerm:                                      │
         │               labelSelector:                                      │
         │                 matchLabels:                                      │
         │                   app: anihpj                                     │
         │               topologyKey: kubernetes.io/hostname                 │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    E.4  Service YAML + Ingress (AWS ALB)
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ # Service (internal)                                              │
         │ apiVersion: v1                                                    │
         │ kind: Service                                                     │
         │ metadata:                                                         │
         │   name: anihpj-service                                            │
         │   namespace: production                                           │
         │ spec:                                                             │
         │   type: NodePort              # ALB uses NodePort mode            │
         │   selector:                                                       │
         │     app: anihpj                                                   │
         │   ports:                                                          │
         │   - port: 80                                                      │
         │     targetPort: 8000                                              │
         │     protocol: TCP                                                 │
         │ ---                                                               │
         │ # Ingress (AWS ALB Controller)                                    │
         │ apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1                                  │
         │ kind: Ingress                                                     │
         │ metadata:                                                         │
         │   name: anihpj-ingress                                            │
         │   namespace: production                                           │
         │   annotations:                                                    │
         │     kubernetes.io/ingress.class: alb                              │
         │     alb.ingress.kubernetes.io/scheme: internet-facing             │
         │     alb.ingress.kubernetes.io/target-type: ip                     │
         │     alb.ingress.kubernetes.io/listen-ports: '[{"HTTPS":443}]'    │
         │     alb.ingress.kubernetes.io/certificate-arn: arn:aws:acm:...   │
         │     alb.ingress.kubernetes.io/healthcheck-path: /health/          │
         │ spec:                                                             │
         │   rules:                                                          │
         │   - host: anihpj.example.com                                      │
         │     http:                                                         │
         │       paths:                                                      │
         │       - path: /                                                   │
         │         pathType: Prefix                                          │
         │         backend:                                                  │
         │           service:                                                │
         │             name: anihpj-service                                  │
         │             port:                                                 │
         │               number: 80                                          │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    E.5  buildspec.yml (AWS CodeBuild)
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ version: 0.2                                                      │
         │ phases:                                                           │
         │   install:                                                        │
         │     runtime-versions:                                             │
         │       python: 3.12                                                │
         │     commands:                                                     │
         │       - pip install --upgrade pip                                 │
         │       - pip install -r requirements.txt                           │
         │   pre_build:                                                      │
         │     commands:                                                     │
         │       - aws ecr get-login-password --region $AWS_REGION | \       │
         │         docker login --username AWS --password-stdin $ECR_URI    │
         │       - export IMAGE_TAG=$(echo $CODEBUILD_RESOLVED_SOURCE_VERSION│
         │         | head -c 8)                                              │
         │   build:                                                          │
         │     commands:                                                     │
         │       - docker build -t $ECR_URI:$IMAGE_TAG .                     │
         │       - docker tag $ECR_URI:$IMAGE_TAG $ECR_URI:latest            │
         │   post_build:                                                     │
         │     commands:                                                     │
         │       - docker push $ECR_URI:$IMAGE_TAG                           │
         │       - docker push $ECR_URI:latest                               │
         │       - printf '{"ImageTag":"%s"}' $IMAGE_TAG > imageDetail.json  │
         │ artifacts:                                                        │
         │   files:                                                          │
         │     - imageDetail.json                                            │
         │     - appspec.yml                                                 │
         │     - deployment.yaml                                             │
         │     - service.yaml                                                │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    E.6  CloudFormation Template (EKS Cluster — Simplified)
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ AWSTemplateFormatVersion: '2010-09-09'                            │
         │ Description: 'EKS Cluster for anihpj production'                  │
         │ Parameters:                                                       │
         │   ClusterName:                                                    │
         │     Type: String                                                  │
         │     Default: anihpj-cluster                                       │
         │   NodeInstanceType:                                               │
         │     Type: String                                                  │
         │     Default: t3.medium                                            │
         │   DesiredCapacity:                                                │
         │     Type: Number                                                  │
         │     Default: 3                                                    │
         │ Resources:                                                        │
         │   EKSCluster:                                                     │
         │     Type: AWS::EKS::Cluster                                       │
         │     Properties:                                                   │
         │       Name: !Ref ClusterName                                      │
         │       Version: "1.30"                                             │
         │       RoleArn: !GetAtt EKSClusterRole.Arn                         │
         │       ResourcesVpcConfig:                                         │
         │         SubnetIds: !Split [",", !ImportValue anihpj-SubnetIds]    │
         │         SecurityGroupIds:                                         │
         │           - !ImportValue anihpj-ClusterSG                         │
         │   EKSNodeGroup:                                                   │
         │     Type: AWS::EKS::Nodegroup                                     │
         │     Properties:                                                   │
         │       ClusterName: !Ref EKSCluster                                │
         │       NodeRole: !GetAtt EKSNodeRole.Arn                           │
         │       Subnets: !Split [",", !ImportValue anihpj-SubnetIds]        │
         │       ScalingConfig:                                              │
         │         DesiredSize: !Ref DesiredCapacity                         │
         │         MinSize: 2                                                │
         │         MaxSize: 10                                               │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


Appendix F: Exam Day Quick Reference Cards
───────────────────────────────────────────
    F.1  Deployment Decision Matrix — "Which deployment strategy?"
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ SCENARIO                         │ STRATEGY       │ AWS SERVICE   │
         │ ─────────────────────────────────┼────────────────┼──────────────│
         │ Zero downtime, instant rollback  │ Blue/Green     │ CodeDeploy    │
         │ Gradual rollout, monitor metrics │ Rolling        │ EKS Deploy    │
         │ Test new version on 10% traffic  │ Canary         │ ALB Weight    │
         │ Deploy to test env, then swap    │ Blue/Green DNS │ Route 53      │
         │ Feature flag, selective rollout  │ Feature Toggle │ AppConfig     │
         │ Replace ALL instances at once    │ All-at-Once    │ CodeDeploy    │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    F.2  Container Service Decision Matrix
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ SCENARIO                         │ AWS SERVICE                    │
         │ ─────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────│
         │ Managed K8s, multi-cloud portable│ EKS (Elastic K8s Service)     │
         │ AWS-only, simpler than K8s       │ ECS (Elastic Container Service)│
         │ No server management at all      │ Fargate (ECS or EKS mode)     │
         │ Simplest container deployment    │ App Runner                     │
         │ On-premises containers           │ ECS Anywhere / EKS Anywhere   │
         │ Serverless, event-driven         │ Lambda (not containers)        │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    F.3  Service Limits You MUST Know for the Exam
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ SERVICE                    │ KEY LIMIT                           │
         │ ──────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────│
         │ S3                        │ Single PUT: 5 GB (multi-part for >)  │
         │ Lambda                    │ 15 min timeout, 10 GB /tmp            │
         │ EKS                      │ Max 5,000 nodes per cluster (quota)   │
         │ ECR                       │ Max 10,000 images per repository      │
         │ CodeBuild                 │ Max 8 hours build timeout            │
         │ CloudFormation           │ Max 500 resources per stack           │
         │ SQS                       │ Max 14 day message retention         │
         │ EventBridge              │ Max 5 targets per rule                │
         │ DynamoDB                  │ Max 400 KB per item                  │
         │ IAM                       │ Max 10 policies per role             │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    F.4  IAM Policy Evaluation Flow
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ 1. Explicit DENY → IMMEDIATELY denied (cannot be overridden)      │
         │ 2. Explicit ALLOW → Allowed (if no matching deny)                │
         │ 3. Implicit DENY → Default (no matching allow = denied)          │
         │                                                                   │
         │ PRIORITY ORDER: DENY > ALLOW > IMPLICIT DENY                      │
         │ SCPs in Organizations can DENY even if IAM policy ALLOWS         │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


Appendix G: Troubleshooting Scenarios Quick Guide
─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    G.1  Pod Won't Start — Diagnostic Flow
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  kubectl get pods                                                │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       ├── STATUS: Pending → Check scheduler                      │
         │       │   kubectl describe pod X | grep -A5 Events               │
         │       │   • "0/3 nodes available" → resource request too high    │
         │       │   • "node(s) had taint" → tolerations needed             │
         │       │   • "PVC not found" → check PersistentVolumeClaim        │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       ├── STATUS: CrashLoopBackOff → Check app startup           │
         │       │   kubectl logs X --previous    # Previous crash logs     │
         │       │   kubectl describe pod X       # Check exit code          │
         │       │   • Exit 1: app error, check logs                        │
         │       │   • Exit 137: OOMKilled (memory limit too low)           │
         │       │   • Exit 143: SIGTERM (graceful shutdown)                │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       ├── STATUS: ImagePullBackOff → Check image                 │
         │       │   • Wrong image tag? (kubectl describe pod X)            │
         │       │   • ECR auth expired? (check IRSA/service account)       │
         │       │   • Private registry? (check imagePullSecrets)           │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       └── STATUS: ErrImagePull → Registry/auth issue             │
         │           • Check docker login / ECR auth token                  │
         │           • Check pod's serviceAccount + IRSA role               │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    G.2  Service Not Accessible — Diagnostic Flow
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  curl http://anihpj-service.production.svc.cluster.local          │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       ├── Connection refused → Pods not running?                 │
         │       │   kubectl get pods -n production -l app=anihpj            │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       ├── Connection timeout → Wrong port? Network policy?        │
         │       │   kubectl get svc -n production anihpj-service           │
         │       │   kubectl get networkpolicies -n production               │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       └── 503 Service Unavailable → Pod not ready?               │
         │           kubectl get endpoints -n production anihpj-service      │
         │           • If no endpoints → readinessProbe failing              │
         │           • Check: kubectl describe pod X (Readiness probe)      │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    G.3  EKS Cluster Issues — Diagnostic Flow
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  kubectl get nodes → "Unable to connect to the server"           │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       ├── AWS CLI context? aws eks update-kubeconfig --name C    │
         │       ├── IAM permissions? aws sts get-caller-identity            │
         │       ├── Cluster active? aws eks describe-cluster --name C       │
         │       │   • Check status: ACTIVE / CREATING / FAILED             │
         │       └── VPC endpoints? (private cluster needs endpoints)        │
         │            • com.amazonaws.region.eks, .ec2, .ecr.api, .ecr.dkr  │
         │            • .sts, .ssm, .logs, .s3                              │
         │                                                                   │
         │  Nodes showing NotReady?                                          │
         │       ├── kubectl describe node X | grep Conditions              │
         │       ├── Check kubelet: journalctl -u kubelet                    │
         │       ├── CNI plugin healthy? kubectl get pods -n kube-system     │
         │       └── aws-node DaemonSet running? (VPC CNI)                   │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    G.4  CodePipeline Failure — Diagnostic Flow
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  Pipeline failed at:                                              │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       ├── Source stage → Check CodeCommit/Repo permissions       │
         │       │   • CodePipeline IAM role has codecommit:Get* ?          │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       ├── Build stage → Check CodeBuild logs                     │
         │       │   • buildspec.yml syntax error?                          │
         │       │   • Docker build failed? (check Dockerfile)              │
         │       │   • ECR push failed? (check IAM role has ecr:Put*)       │
         │       │                                                           │
         │       └── Deploy stage → Check CodeDeploy                        │
         │           • appspec.yml valid?                                    │
         │           • EKS cluster reachable? (check kubeconfig)            │
         │           • CodeDeploy service account has RBAC permissions?     │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


Appendix H: AWS DevOps Certification — Final Cheat Sheet
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    H.1  All Key AWS Services in One Table
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ SERVICE              │ CATEGORY      │ KEY EXAM FACT               │
         │ ────────────────────┼───────────────┼─────────────────────────────│
         │ CodePipeline         │ CI/CD         │ Orchestrates 4 stages       │
         │ CodeBuild            │ CI/CD         │ buildspec.yml in repo root  │
         │ CodeDeploy           │ CI/CD         │ Blue/Green, Canary, Rolling │
         │ CodeCommit           │ Source        │ AWS-managed Git repos       │
         │ CloudFormation       │ IaC           │ !Ref, !Sub, !Join, !Select  │
         │ CDK                  │ IaC           │ TypeScript/Python → CFN     │
         │ CloudWatch           │ Monitoring    │ Metrics, Logs, Alarms, Dash │
         │ CloudTrail           │ Auditing      │ API activity audit trail    │
         │ AWS Config           │ Compliance    │ Resource history + rules    │
         │ X-Ray                │ Tracing       │ Distributed tracing         │
         │ EKS                  │ Containers    │ Managed K8s control plane   │
         │ ECS                  │ Containers    │ AWS-native container svc    │
         │ Fargate              │ Serverless    │ No server management        │
         │ ECR                  │ Registry      │ Container image storage     │
         │ S3                   │ Storage       │ 11 9s durability            │
         │ EBS                  │ Storage       │ Block storage for EC2       │
         │ EFS                  │ Storage       │ NFS for multi-AZ sharing    │
         │ RDS                  │ Database      │ Managed relational DB       │
         │ DynamoDB             │ Database      │ Serverless NoSQL            │
         │ ElastiCache          │ Cache         │ Redis/Memcached             │
         │ Route 53             │ DNS           │ Latency/Failover/Geo/Weight │
         │ CloudFront           │ CDN           │ Global edge caching         │
         │ ALB                  │ LB            │ Layer 7 (HTTP/HTTPS)        │
         │ NLB                  │ LB            │ Layer 4 (TCP/UDP/TLS)       │
         │ IAM                  │ Security      │ Users, Roles, Policies      │
         │ KMS                  │ Security      │ Encryption key management   │
         │ Secrets Manager      │ Security      │ Auto-rotate secrets         │
         │ GuardDuty            │ Security      │ AI threat detection         │
         │ Security Hub         │ Security      │ Centralized security view   │
         │ WAF                  │ Security      │ Web app firewall            │
         │ Shield               │ Security      │ DDoS protection             │
         │ SNS                  │ Messaging     │ Pub/sub notifications       │
         │ SQS                  │ Messaging     │ Message queuing (decouple)  │
         │ EventBridge          │ Events        │ Event bus + rules           │
         │ Step Functions       │ Workflow      │ Visual state machines       │
         │ SSM                  │ Management    │ Patch, automate, no SSH     │
         │ Organizations        │ Governance    │ Multi-account management    │
         │ Trusted Advisor      │ Cost/Ops      │ Best practice checks        │
         │ Auto Scaling         │ Compute       │ EC2 dynamic scaling         │
         │ Kinesis              │ Streaming     │ Real-time data streaming    │
         │ Lambda               │ Compute       │ Serverless functions        │
         │ API Gateway          │ API Mgmt      │ REST/HTTP API frontend      │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    H.2  S3 Storage Class Comparison
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ CLASS               │ ACCESS        │ RETRIEVAL    │ USE CASE     │
         │ ────────────────────┼───────────────┼──────────────┼─────────────│
         │ Standard            │ Frequent      │ Instant      │ Active data  │
         │ Intelligent-Tiering │ Auto-moving   │ Instant      │ Unknown pat  │
         │ Standard-IA         │ Infrequent    │ Instant/fee  │ Backups      │
         │ One Zone-IA         │ Infrequent    │ Instant/fee  │ Recreatable  │
         │ Glacier Instant     │ Quarterly     │ Minutes      │ Archive-med  │
         │ Glacier Flexible    │ Yearly        │ Hours        │ Archive      │
         │ Glacier Deep Archive│ 7+ years      │ 12-48 hours  │ Compliance   │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    H.3  Load Balancer Comparison
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ TYPE │ LAYER │ PROTOCOL       │ USE CASE                         │
         │ ─────┼───────┼────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────│
         │ ALB  │ 7     │ HTTP/HTTPS     │ Web apps, microservices, EKS     │
         │ NLB  │ 4     │ TCP/UDP/TLS    │ Extreme perf, static IPs, gRPC   │
         │ GWLB │ 3     │ IP             │ Virtual appliances (firewalls)    │
         │ CLB  │ 4/7   │ HTTP/TCP       │ Legacy (use ALB/NLB instead)     │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    H.4  EBS Volume Type Comparison
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │ TYPE  │ MAX IOPS    │ MAX THROUGHPUT │ USE CASE                  │
         │ ──────┼─────────────┼─────────────────┼───────────────────────── │
         │ gp3   │ 16,000      │ 1,000 MB/s      │ General purpose (default)│
         │ gp2   │ 16,000      │ 250 MB/s        │ Legacy (burst credits)   │
         │ io2   │ 256,000     │ 4,000 MB/s      │ I/O intensive (99.999%)  │
         │ io1   │ 64,000      │ 1,000 MB/s      │ I/O intensive (99.9%)    │
         │ st1   │ 500         │ 500 MB/s        │ Big data, throughput     │
         │ sc1   │ 250         │ 250 MB/s        │ Cold data, cheapest      │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
